# SysML v1 to v2 transformation census

OMG's SysML v1 to v2 transformation model names every mapping it applies — one class per
mapping, grouped by the v1 metamodel package it migrates. This page is the census of those
mapping classes against OpenSysML's migrator: each row ties one OMG mapping to the code that
carries it out, or records why it does not.

**Run:** `go run -C tools ./cmd/transformation-census` (rewrites the generated blocks below from the
baseline); `go run -C tools ./cmd/transformation-census -check` (the gate); `-update` re-extracts the
mapping list from the pinned model keeping every recorded verdict; `-measure` recomputes the scope
counts.
**Baseline:** [sysml-v1-transformation-census-baseline.json](sysml-v1-transformation-census-baseline.json)
**Model:** provisioned by `./scripts/download-sysml-v1tov2.sh` into `build/sysml-v1tov2/`, pinned by
`scripts/sysml-v1tov2-pin.sh`; `-check` compares against it when present and `-check -require-xmi`
fails when absent.

## Reading a row

Each row's status says what the migrator does with the v1 element the OMG mapping covers:

- **✅ faithful** — the migrator writes the OMG target, or the v2-equivalent shape of it.
- **⚠️ approximate** — the migrator writes it with a documented loss and ledgers the element as
  approximated.
- **❌ not implemented** — the migrator does not write it.
- **⛔ deliberate** — the migrator intentionally writes something different; the reason says why.
- **🚧 known failure** — the mapping is attempted but comes out wrong.
- **❔ unknown** — the verdict is not settled; the reason names what is missing.

Sub-mappings — the `…Membership`, `…FeatureTyping`, `…Redefinition` and `…FeatureValue` classes
that build one relationship of a target — share the verdict of the construct they belong to.

}`); a fUML or Alf library behavior, which v2 has no type for, computes through the v2 library instead (see the behaviours with no OMG mapping); a block-owned behavior that reads its block is written `perform action n ::> obj.usage;`, since a def nested in a part def reaches none of the block's features; a callee owned by another block gets a bound `context` parameter, bound to the caller's object or to the caller's one part of that block (`bind n.context = …;` or `action n : B { in ref :>> context = …; }`); each per-call loss stays in the ledger under its own row or reason: edge notes such as several edges leaving the node, which a fork carries (a node no edge leads to is started by the activity, which is mapped), a context left unbound where the caller holds no object of the callee's block (performed as an empty step), and arguments missing or empty in the v1 model (PSSM: 304 mapped, 849 approximated, 6 unmapped of 1159; of the approximated, 831 carry edge notes and 9 only those; 580 library calls, 199 usage performances, 110 contexts bound to a part, 206 empty steps, 170 calls missing arguments) |

## Migrator behaviour with no OMG mapping

The migrator also does things no OMG mapping class covers — in both directions: behaviours it
performs that the model never asks for, and outputs it produces where the model is silent. Each
entry names the behaviour and the code and test that carry it out.

<!-- census:begin beyond -->
| Migrator behaviour | Implementation | Test |
|---|---|---|
| MagicDraw/Cameo export quirks: tool profiles (MD Customization, UML Standard Profile, Validation) skipped by exact path; standard stereotypes recognised by provenance | `internal/translate/migrate/stereotypes.go:isStandard` | `tests/migrate/profile_test.go:TestToolProfilesAreSkippedByExactPathOnly` |
| Element-by-element migration ledger (mapped / approximated / unmapped / skipped with a reason per element) | `internal/translate/migrate/migrate.go:add` | `tests/migrate/migrate_test.go:TestReportAccountsForEveryElement` |
| MTIP diagram layout carried onto views as layout metadata and routes | `internal/translate/migrate/streams.go:layoutRecord` | `tests/migrate/layout_test.go:TestGoldenLayout` |
| Diagrams written as views exposing what they show | `internal/translate/migrate/diagrams.go:planViews` | `internal/translate/migrate/diagrams_internal_test.go:TestDiagramViews` |
| DocGen documents, sections, tables and expression columns lowered to executable views | `internal/translate/migrate/documents.go:planDocuments` | `tests/migrate/documents_test.go:TestMigratedDocumentsRender` |
| Cameo simulation configurations written as runnable action defs; result snapshots and Monte Carlo summaries | `internal/translate/migrate/simconfig.go:simulationConfig` | `tests/migrate/simconfig_test.go:TestSimulationConfigBecomesARunnableActionDef` |
| Pasted images in comments and documents written as image paragraphs from the archive | `internal/translate/migrate/documents.go:planImage` | `tests/migrate/images_test.go:TestImageParagraphsFromArchive` |
| View Editor cross-references in prose resolved to v2 references | `internal/translate/migrate/references.go:resolveRef` | `tests/migrate/cross_references_test.go:TestMigratedCrossReferences` |
| Deferred events (State::deferrableTrigger) kept and replayed through DeferredEvent / DeferredKeeper metadata; the OMG model has no mapping for deferrable triggers | `internal/translate/migrate/states.go:deferredHead` | `tests/migrate/behavior_more_test.go:TestStrictDeferredSignalsAreKeptAndReplayed` |
| Extend and ExtensionPoint: written as a dependency on the extended use case (v2 has no extend); the OMG model has no mapping for them | `internal/translate/migrate/usecase.go:extend` | `tests/migrate/migrate_test.go:TestGoldenNotation` |
| fUML / Alf library behaviors called by href computed through the v2 library | `internal/translate/migrate/activity.go:callBehavior` | `tests/migrate/library_test.go:TestLibraryCallsComputeThroughTheV2Library` |
<!-- census:end beyond -->

## Candidate errata

These rows name OMG mappings that look inconsistent with the SysML v2 specification. Nothing is
filed upstream; the note on each row says what the mapping writes and what the specification
expects instead.

<!-- census:begin errata -->
| OMG mapping | v1 source | v2 target | Status | Note |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `CommonReturnParameterFeatureMembership_Mapping` | `Element` | `ReturnParameterMembership` | ⛔ deliberate | the mapping creates a ReturnParameterMembership whose owner is an ActionDefinition, which validateReturnParameterMembershipOwningType rejects; an `out` ParameterMembership is the valid form for an action def's result |
| `CommonReturnParameterReferenceUsageMembership_Mapping` | `Element` | `ReturnParameterMembership` | ⛔ deliberate | the mapping creates a ReturnParameterMembership whose owner is an ActionDefinition, which validateReturnParameterMembershipOwningType rejects; an `out` ParameterMembership is the valid form for an action def's result |
| `Stakeholder_Mapping` | `Class` | `ItemDefinition` | ✅ faithful | the mapping maps «Stakeholder» to an ItemDefinition, but a v2 stakeholder parameter is a PartUsage (StakeholderMembership::ownedStakeholderParameter), which requires a PartDefinition among its definitions; a PartDefinition is the type that can be used |
| `ViewpointRenderingUsageActionUsageFeatureTyping_Mapping` | `Class` | `FeatureTyping` | ✅ faithful | this mapping has no type operation, while ViewpointRenderingUsage_Mapping passes «Create» Operations to mappings whose from is a Class; the migrator types each rendering action by the operation method (or its action def when no method is written) and by the method tag behaviors. |
| `AcceptCallAction_Mapping` | `AcceptCallAction` | `AcceptActionUsage` | ⛔ deliberate | the mapping specializes AcceptEventAction_Mapping, whose AcceptActionUsage needs a trigger v2 cannot write for a CallEvent, while its note says the action is mapped to an empty action usage to keep its connections; the empty action usage is written |
| `COAOutputPinFeatureChainExpressionMembership_Mapping` | `OutputPin` | `Membership` | ✅ faithful | COAOutputPinFeatureChainExpression_Mapping values the output pin's parameter with the feature chain `target.op` (and COAOutputPinFeatureReferenceExpression_Mapping with `target`), which evaluates to the operation's performance (or the target object), not the operation's result; the valid form reads the operation's own `out` parameter, `target.op.p`, or redefines it in the call |
| `COAOutputPinFeatureChainExpression_Mapping` | `OutputPin` | `FeatureChainExpression` | ✅ faithful | COAOutputPinFeatureChainExpression_Mapping values the output pin's parameter with the feature chain `target.op` (and COAOutputPinFeatureReferenceExpression_Mapping with `target`), which evaluates to the operation's performance (or the target object), not the operation's result; the valid form reads the operation's own `out` parameter, `target.op.p`, or redefines it in the call |
| `COAOutputPinFeatureFeatureMembership_Mapping` | `OutputPin` | `FeatureMembership` | ✅ faithful | COAOutputPinFeatureChainExpression_Mapping values the output pin's parameter with the feature chain `target.op` (and COAOutputPinFeatureReferenceExpression_Mapping with `target`), which evaluates to the operation's performance (or the target object), not the operation's result; the valid form reads the operation's own `out` parameter, `target.op.p`, or redefines it in the call |
| `COAOutputPinFeatureFeatureValue_Mapping` | `OutputPin` | `FeatureValue` | ✅ faithful | COAOutputPinFeatureChainExpression_Mapping values the output pin's parameter with the feature chain `target.op` (and COAOutputPinFeatureReferenceExpression_Mapping with `target`), which evaluates to the operation's performance (or the target object), not the operation's result; the valid form reads the operation's own `out` parameter, `target.op.p`, or redefines it in the call |
| `COAOutputPinFeatureFeature_Mapping` | `OutputPin` | `Feature` | ✅ faithful | COAOutputPinFeatureChainExpression_Mapping values the output pin's parameter with the feature chain `target.op` (and COAOutputPinFeatureReferenceExpression_Mapping with `target`), which evaluates to the operation's performance (or the target object), not the operation's result; the valid form reads the operation's own `out` parameter, `target.op.p`, or redefines it in the call |
| `COAOutputPinFeatureMembership_Mapping` | `OutputPin` | `FeatureMembership` | ✅ faithful | COAOutputPinFeatureChainExpression_Mapping values the output pin's parameter with the feature chain `target.op` (and COAOutputPinFeatureReferenceExpression_Mapping with `target`), which evaluates to the operation's performance (or the target object), not the operation's result; the valid form reads the operation's own `out` parameter, `target.op.p`, or redefines it in the call |
| `COAOutputPinFeatureReferenceExpressionMembership_Mapping` | `OutputPin` | `Membership` | ✅ faithful | COAOutputPinFeatureChainExpression_Mapping values the output pin's parameter with the feature chain `target.op` (and COAOutputPinFeatureReferenceExpression_Mapping with `target`), which evaluates to the operation's performance (or the target object), not the operation's result; the valid form reads the operation's own `out` parameter, `target.op.p`, or redefines it in the call |
| `COAOutputPinFeatureReferenceExpression_Mapping` | `OutputPin` | `FeatureReferenceExpression` | ✅ faithful | COAOutputPinFeatureChainExpression_Mapping values the output pin's parameter with the feature chain `target.op` (and COAOutputPinFeatureReferenceExpression_Mapping with `target`), which evaluates to the operation's performance (or the target object), not the operation's result; the valid form reads the operation's own `out` parameter, `target.op.p`, or redefines it in the call |
| `COAOutputPinFeature_Mapping` | `OutputPin` | `Feature` | ✅ faithful | COAOutputPinFeatureChainExpression_Mapping values the output pin's parameter with the feature chain `target.op` (and COAOutputPinFeatureReferenceExpression_Mapping with `target`), which evaluates to the operation's performance (or the target object), not the operation's result; the valid form reads the operation's own `out` parameter, `target.op.p`, or redefines it in the call |
| `COAOutputPinParameterMembership_Mapping` | `OutputPin` | `ParameterMembership` | ✅ faithful | COAOutputPinFeatureChainExpression_Mapping values the output pin's parameter with the feature chain `target.op` (and COAOutputPinFeatureReferenceExpression_Mapping with `target`), which evaluates to the operation's performance (or the target object), not the operation's result; the valid form reads the operation's own `out` parameter, `target.op.p`, or redefines it in the call |
| `COAOutputPinReferenceUsageFeatureValue_Mapping` | `OutputPin` | `FeatureValue` | ✅ faithful | COAOutputPinFeatureChainExpression_Mapping values the output pin's parameter with the feature chain `target.op` (and COAOutputPinFeatureReferenceExpression_Mapping with `target`), which evaluates to the operation's performance (or the target object), not the operation's result; the valid form reads the operation's own `out` parameter, `target.op.p`, or redefines it in the call |
| `COAOutputPinReferenceUsage_Mapping` | `OutputPin` | `ReferenceUsage` | ✅ faithful | COAOutputPinFeatureChainExpression_Mapping values the output pin's parameter with the feature chain `target.op` (and COAOutputPinFeatureReferenceExpression_Mapping with `target`), which evaluates to the operation's performance (or the target object), not the operation's result; the valid form reads the operation's own `out` parameter, `target.op.p`, or redefines it in the call |
| `SSAItemReferenceUsageInvocationExpression_Mapping` | `InvocationAction` | `InvocationExpression` | ✅ faithful | the mapping values the payload with an InvocationExpression typed by the signal, an item definition, which validation refuses (`Must invoke a behavior or a behavioral feature`, by our validator and the pilot's alike), and passes none of the action's arguments; a ConstructorExpression (`new S(…)`) is the valid form for an instance of an item definition, and carries the arguments |
| `SSAReferenceUsage_Mapping` | `InvocationAction` | `ReferenceUsage` | ✅ faithful | the mapping drops the action's onPort, so a send through a port becomes a send from the object to the target; `via` the port, which binds the sender, is the v2 form for sending through it, and is written |
| `SendObjectAction_Mapping` | `SendObjectAction` | `ActionUsage` | ✅ faithful | SSAItemReferenceUsageFeatureTyping_Mapping, which SendObjectAction_Mapping inherits through SendSignalAction_Mapping, types the sent item by `from.request`, an InputPin, which is no Type, so the FeatureTyping is ill-formed; the valid form sends the pin's value, `send request to target` |
| `COAInvocationExpression_Mapping` | `CreateObjectAction` | `InvocationExpression` | ⛔ deliberate | the mapping writes an InvocationExpression of the created classifier, which validation rejects for a classifier that is not a behavior or behavioral feature; an InstantiationExpression (`new C()`) is the v2 form that creates an instance |
| `DOADestroyActionUsageReferenceUsage_Mapping` | `DestroyObjectAction` | `ReferenceUsage` | ✅ faithful | the mapping types an ActionUsage by the function OccurrenceFunctions::destroy and adds a ReferenceUsage that redefines none of its parameters, so `occ` is never bound and nothing is destroyed; invoking the function on the target, which binds `occ`, is written |
| `RSAOutputPin_Mapping` | `OutputPin` | `ReferenceUsage` | ✅ faithful | the mapping marks the result pin abstract (isAbstract = true) while binding it to `this` with a FeatureValue, but an abstract feature's values are those of the features specializing it and the pin has none, so the binding contradicts it; the pin is written as a concrete `out` feature |
| `VSAOutputPinFeatureValue_Mapping` | `OutputPin` | `FeatureValue` | ✅ faithful | the mapping binds the value as v1 stores it, so a typed-in string on a scalar pin (a LiteralString "2" on an Integer result) becomes a String FeatureValue of an Integer feature, which v2 typing rejects (`cannot bind String value to a feature typed by Integer`); the literal is written as the scalar the pin holds (`out result : Integer[1] = 2;`) |
| `RaiseExceptionAction_Mapping` | `RaiseExceptionAction` | `ActionUsage` | ✅ faithful | the mapping gives only an ActionUsage, and SysML v2 has no exceptions or handlers; with no handler a raised exception ends the activity, so the action is followed by a terminate action |
| `ReduceAction_Mapping` | `ReduceAction` | `ActionUsage` | ✅ faithful | the mapping gives only an ActionUsage with no defined content, which drops the reduction; the result is written as `collection->reduce {in x; in y; F(x, y)}` over the reducer's v2 function (KerML ControlFunctions::reduce) |
| `LoopNode_Mapping` | `LoopNode` | `ActionUsage` | ⚠️ approximate | the mapping gives only an ActionUsage and leaves its details undefined, so a loop's test and iteration are lost |
| `AddVariableValueAction_Mapping` | `AddVariableValueAction` | `ActionUsage` | ✅ faithful | the mapping's ownedRelationship omits Helper.actionOwnedRelationship (which ClearVariableAction_Mapping and RemoveVariableValueAction_Mapping include), so the action's value and insertAt pins — the value added — are not written; the action is written with its pins and an assignment to the variable (`assign v := SequenceFunctions::including(v, value);`, `includingAt` with insertAt, `:= value` when replacing all) |
| `ControlNodeObjectFlowReferenceUsage_Mapping` | `ObjectFlow` | `ReferenceUsage` | ✅ faithful | the `outputObject` branch of the OCL tests `from.target.oclIsTypeOf(UML::MergeNode)` where it means `from.source`, which is written |
| `JoinMergeNodeObjectFlowOperatorExpression_Mapping` | `ObjectFlow` | `OperatorExpression` | ✅ faithful | a merge forwards each arriving value on its own rather than the sequence of every input, which is written |
| `Slot_Mapping` | `Slot` | `Feature` | ✅ faithful | the mapping creates a KerML Feature; a SysML redefinition of the classifier's feature is a Usage (AttributeUsage / ReferenceUsage / PartUsage per the feature) |
| `Constraint_Mapping` | `Constraint` | `ConstraintDefinition` | ⛔ deliberate | the mapping puts the specification in an owned calc feature with a return parameter (ElementFeatureMembership + CommonReturnParameterReferenceUsageMembership) rather than as the def's result expression, so a def written literally would not test the specification |
| `InformationFlow_Mapping` | `InformationFlow` | `FlowDefinition` | ✅ faithful | the mapping creates a FlowDefinition for a v1 information flow between specific sources and targets; a definition has no ends bound to those features, which a FlowUsage would have; an information flow realized only by an association is not migrated (PSSM 0, fixtures 0, TMT 0) |
| `CombinedFragment_Mapping` | `CombinedFragment` | `Interaction` | ⛔ deliberate | the mapping creates an Interaction, a definition, for a fragment that occurs once inside its owning interaction; an occurrence usage would be the matching kind |
| `InteractionUse_Mapping` | `InteractionUse` | `Step` | ✅ faithful | the mapping creates a KerML Step; the SysML counterpart of performing another behavior is a PerformActionUsage, which is written |
| `Message_Mapping` | `Message` | `Flow` | ⛔ deliberate | the mapping creates a KerML Flow; in a SysML v2 model a message is a FlowUsage (the `message` declaration), which is a Usage and can be owned by definitions and usages |
| `StateInvariant_Mapping` | `StateInvariant` | `Invariant` | ✅ faithful | the mapping creates a KerML Invariant; the SysML counterpart is an AssertConstraintUsage, which is written |
| `ElementImport_Mapping` | `ElementImport` | `MembershipImport` | ✅ faithful | the mapping sets `importedMemberName` to the alias, which a MembershipImport does not have; an aliased element import is written as an alias membership `alias A for P::X` |
| `PackageURIFeatureTyping_Mapping` | `Package` | `FeatureTyping` | ⛔ deliberate | the mapping types the URI metadata usage by `PackageData`, an attribute definition, but a metadata usage must be typed by a metadata definition |
| `StereotypeOccurenceUsage_Mapping` | `Stereotype` | `OccurrenceUsage` | ⛔ deliberate | the occurrence usage is typed through a mapping class the model does not define and is owned by no mapping |
| `ExpressionElse_Mapping` | `Expression` | `OperatorExpression` | ✅ faithful | the mapping writes an OperatorExpression with operator `else` (a SysMLv1 textual representation), and no library function is named else, so the expression is ill-formed; v2 writes an else guard as the `else` succession of a decision or the unguarded branch of a choice |
<!-- census:end errata -->

Editorially, several class names in the pinned model are misspelled — `COAInvocationExpessionFeatureTyping_Mapping`,
`ElementGroupMetadaMembership_Mapping` and the `StereotypeOccurence…_Mapping` family — and the census records them
verbatim, since a corrected spelling would name a class the model does not contain.

## Gaps ranked

Approximate and not-implemented rows grouped by shared scope, ranked by how many elements of the
pinned PSSM suite plus the repository's migration fixtures carry the scope tokens — `uml:X`
counts elements whose xmi:type is `uml:X`, `sysml:X` counts stereotype applications named X in a
SysML profile namespace. The counts are elements affected, not effort.
